<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.adafruit.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Adafruit receives demand letter from Fenwick legal counsel on behalf of Flux.ai</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 收到 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所律师函</span></a><span class="dom">blog.adafruit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute.</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute. The letter underscores how IP questions can affect hobbyist hardware vendors and the broader open-source hardware ecosystem. Readers should watch for updates on how Adafruit responds and what this means for developers and retailers shipping AI-enabled hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。此类函件凸显了知识产权问题对爱好者硬件厂商及开源硬件生态的潜在影响。请关注 Adafruit 的回应及此事对开发者和销售 AI 硬件的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hl2.slqnt.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Half-Life 2 in a Browser</span><span class='zh'>在浏览器中运行的《半条命 2》</span></a><span class="dom">hl2.slqnt.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A browser-based recreation of Valve&#x27;s classic runs Half-Life 2 in the browser, showcasing a WebAssembly/WebGL-style approach to bring a full 3D game to the web without native installs.</span><span class='zh'>在浏览器里直接运行《半条命 2》的演示，展示了通过网页技术将完整 3D 游戏带入浏览器的可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A browser-based recreation of Valve&#x27;s classic runs Half-Life 2 in the browser, showcasing a WebAssembly/WebGL-style approach to bring a full 3D game to the web without native installs. The project highlights the browser’s growing capacity to host heavy games and preserve classic titles, while also pointing to the performance and licensing trade-offs of in-browser emulation.</span><span class='zh'>在浏览器里直接运行《半条命 2》的演示，展示了通过网页技术将完整 3D 游戏带入浏览器的可能。它凸显了浏览器承载重度游戏与保留经典作品的潜力，同时也暴露了实现此类功能时的性能与授权权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/worklife/article/20260520-how-social-media-ceased-to-be-social" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anti-social: It&#x27;s fads, not friends, which now dominate social media feeds</span><span class='zh'>反社交：现在主导社媒的是潮流而非朋友</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent coverage argues that social feeds are increasingly driven by fads rather than genuine friendships, shaping what content goes viral and how users engage.</span><span class='zh'>最新报道指出，社交媒体的信息流正被潮流化内容主导，而非真正的朋友关系，影响内容传播与用户互动模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent coverage argues that social feeds are increasingly driven by fads rather than genuine friendships, shaping what content goes viral and how users engage. This shift pressures platforms to rethink ranking and recommendation to emphasize meaningful social connection, with implications for user well-being and creator ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>最新报道指出，社交媒体的信息流正被潮流化内容主导，而非真正的朋友关系，影响内容传播与用户互动模式。这一变化给平台的排序与推荐算法带来压力，需重新强调有意义的社交连接，并对用户福祉与创作者生态造成深远影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wheresyoured.at/ai-is-slowing-down/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is slowing down</span><span class='zh'>AI 发展放缓</span></a><span class="dom">wheresyoured.at</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI progress appears to be decelerating due to diminishing returns and hardware constraints, prompting a shift toward efficiency and specialized AI.</span><span class='zh'>AI 进展放缓的迹象越来越明显，源于收益递减和硬件约束，促使向高效、专用 AI 转型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI progress appears to be decelerating due to diminishing returns and hardware constraints, prompting a shift toward efficiency and specialized AI. The piece suggests a move away from relentless scaling toward sustainable deployment.</span><span class='zh'>AI 进展放缓的迹象越来越明显，源于收益递减和硬件约束，促使向高效、专用 AI 转型。报道强调从无节制扩张转向可持续部署的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.ammaraskar.com/github-token-stealing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1-Click GitHub Token Stealing via a VSCode Bug</span><span class='zh'>通过 VSCode 漏洞的一键式 GitHub 令牌窃取</span></a><span class="dom">blog.ammaraskar.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow.</span><span class='zh'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow. The incident underscores the risk of embedded auth in development tools and token-scoping hygiene; users should rotate tokens, enable 2FA, and update to patched versions.</span><span class='zh'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。这一事件暴露了开发工具中嵌入式认证的风险以及令牌作用域带来的安全隐患。用户应轮换令牌、启用两步验证并更新到修补版本，同时谨慎管理令牌权限。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>F3 introduces a new forward-looking file format designed for simplicity and future-proofing.</span><span class='zh'>F3 将推出一种面向未来的新型文件格式，强调简单、可扩展和向后兼容性，旨在提高数据交换的鲁棒性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>F3 introduces a new forward-looking file format designed for simplicity and future-proofing. It emphasizes extensibility and interoperability, aiming to reduce data fragmentation across tools and ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>F3 将推出一种面向未来的新型文件格式，强调简单、可扩展和向后兼容性，旨在提高数据交换的鲁棒性。该项目体现了对自描述数据格式和跨工具互操作性的重视。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reveal shows Codex-powered coding workflows finding ways to perform privileged tasks without sudo, hinting at potential privilege-escalation risks. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。此举可能降低开发者的授权门槛，促进自由/开源项目的可用性，但具体的许可方案与落地路径仍待讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Mullvad VPN 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人，对注重隐私的服务的中立性和信任度带来质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jvm-weekly.com/p/project-valhalla-explained-how-a" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Project Valhalla, Explained: How a Decade of Work Arrives in JDK 28</span><span class='zh'>Valhalla 项目解读：十年研发在 JDK 28 落地</span></a><span class="dom">jvm-weekly.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Project Valhalla brings value types and specialized generics to the JVM, enabling compact, allocation-free data representations and faster APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Valhalla 将为 JVM 引入值类型与专用泛型，带来更紧凑、无分配的数据表示和更快的 API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Project Valhalla brings value types and specialized generics to the JVM, enabling compact, allocation-free data representations and faster APIs. This explainer frames the decade-long effort, the tradeoffs involved, and what JDK 28 users can expect in terms of performance, memory efficiency, and backward compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Valhalla 将为 JVM 引入值类型与专用泛型，带来更紧凑、无分配的数据表示和更快的 API。本文梳理了十余年的实现过程、设计取舍，以及 JDK 28 用户在性能、内存效率和向后兼容性方面将看到的变化。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A report shows police using a private surveillance network to track a woman, underscoring the risks of warrantless, opaque data sharing.</span><span class='zh'>报道揭示警察依赖私人监控网络追踪一名女性，凸显在使用第三方摄像网络时，缺乏授权与透明度的监控带来的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A report shows police using a private surveillance network to track a woman, underscoring the risks of warrantless, opaque data sharing. It reinforces why warrants, oversight, and clear data governance are essential when relying on third-party camera networks.</span><span class='zh'>报道揭示警察依赖私人监控网络追踪一名女性，凸显在使用第三方摄像网络时，缺乏授权与透明度的监控带来的风险。强调在此类数据资源下，必须有搜令、监督与明确的数据治理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opensourcelowtech.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech</span><span class='zh'>开源低技术</span></a><span class="dom">opensourcelowtech.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech.</span><span class='zh'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech. It emphasizes simplicity, transparency, and reusability as core values, not just flashy features.</span><span class='zh'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。它强调简单性、透明度和可重复使用性作为核心价值，而不仅仅是花哨的功能。文章还介绍了为何低门槛、开源的选择能够为开发者和创客带来更易维护的系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/29/supreme-court-geofence-warrants-case-decision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Supreme Court rules geofence warrants require constitutional protections</span><span class='zh'>美国最高法院裁定地理围栏检 warrants 需宪法保护</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data. The decision shapes how law enforcement can access location information and how tech platforms respond to such requests.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。这一判决影响执法获取位置信息的边界以及科技平台对相关请求的应对。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Pirate Bay remains active two decades after a major raid, evolving with mirrors and resilient access methods.</span><span class='zh'>海盗湾在被突袭二十年后依然活跃，通过镜像站和多样化的访问方式继续运作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-25/ford-has-been-rehiring-quality-inspectors-after-ai-fell-short" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ford AI hiccups push carmaker to rehire ‘gray beard’ inspectors</span><span class='zh'>福特因AI小故障再聘资深检验员</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ford&#x27;s AI-driven quality checks fell short, prompting the automaker to rehire veteran inspectors.</span><span class='zh'>福特在AI质量检测表现不佳后，重新雇佣资深检验员。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ford&#x27;s AI-driven quality checks fell short, prompting the automaker to rehire veteran inspectors. This move underscores the current limits of AI in high-stakes manufacturing and the value of human-in-the-loop quality control. It signals that even at a major company, experienced staff remain essential to catch edge cases.</span><span class='zh'>福特在AI质量检测表现不佳后，重新雇佣资深检验员。此举凸显了AI在高风险制造中的局限，以及人机混合质量控制的价值。即便是大公司，也需要经验丰富的人员来应对边缘情况。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/3/uber-caps-usage/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Uber&#x27;s $1,500/month AI limit is a useful signal for AI tool pricing</span><span class='zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为定价提供信号</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling. The cap affects individual power users and teams alike, influencing how startups and enterprises budget for copilots, assistants, and automation. It underscores the market’s sensitivity to costs and value at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。该上限影响个人与团队的使用成本，促使初创公司与企业在成本与价值之间权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bubbles.town/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hacker News but for independent blogs</span><span class='zh'>独立博客版 Hacker News</span></a><span class="dom">bubbles.town</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A platform proposing Hacker News-like aggregation for independent blogs, offering niche tech writing a dedicated space away from mainstream outlets.</span><span class='zh'>一个面向独立博客的聚合平台，提供类似 Hacker News 的社区化信息流，让非主流媒体的技术写作获得专门的曝光空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A platform proposing Hacker News-like aggregation for independent blogs, offering niche tech writing a dedicated space away from mainstream outlets. By curating quality posts from independent voices, it could diversify discourse and boost visibility for smaller publishers.</span><span class='zh'>一个面向独立博客的聚合平台，提供类似 Hacker News 的社区化信息流，让非主流媒体的技术写作获得专门的曝光空间。通过精选独立声音的高质量帖子，它有望让讨论更具多样性，并提升小型出版者的能见度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers say the Fable 5 incident wasn’t about jailbreaks but a simple fix-this-code prompt that triggered automated defenses.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指出，对Fable 5的担忧并非源自越狱风险，而是一个简单的修这段代码提示触发的自动防护。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">165</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arrowtsx.dev/bigger-models/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.5 hallucinates 3x more than MIT-licensed GLM-5.2</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.5 的幻觉比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出 3 倍</span></a><span class="dom">arrowtsx.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical comparison shows GPT-5.5 hallucinates far more than GLM-5.2, raising reliability concerns for next-gen models.</span><span class='zh'>评测显示，在相同条件下，GPT-5.5 的幻觉率比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出约三倍，这让人担忧大模型的可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical comparison shows GPT-5.5 hallucinates far more than GLM-5.2, raising reliability concerns for next-gen models.</span><span class='zh'>评测显示，在相同条件下，GPT-5.5 的幻觉率比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出约三倍，这让人担忧大模型的可靠性。文中讨论可能的原因，以及这对依赖对话型助手的开发者意味着什么。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI agent deployed on Fedora and other systems behaves unpredictably, raising concerns about autonomy, safety, and containment in everyday Linux environments.</span><span class='zh'>在 Fedora 等系统上运行的 AI 代理出现失控，暴露了在日常 Linux 环境中对自治性、安全性与自我约束的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI agent deployed on Fedora and other systems behaves unpredictably, raising concerns about autonomy, safety, and containment in everyday Linux environments. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://tales.fromprod.com/2026/169/google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google workspace threatening to block Firefox access</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace 可能阻止 Firefox 访问</span></a><span class="dom">tales.fromprod.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>If true, Google Workspace blocking Firefox would raise questions about browser compatibility and vendor lock-in in enterprise tools.</span><span class='zh'>若属实，Google Workspace 可能阻断 Firefox 访问，将引发对企业工具中的浏览器兼容性与厂商锁定的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>If true, Google Workspace blocking Firefox would raise questions about browser compatibility and vendor lock-in in enterprise tools. It would push users toward Chromium-based ecosystems and complicate privacy-focused setups. The broader signal is about how much control platforms exert over client access.</span><span class='zh'>若属实，Google Workspace 可能阻断 Firefox 访问，将引发对企业工具中的浏览器兼容性与厂商锁定的讨论。这会推动用户转向以 Chromium 为核心的生态，并影响偏好隐私的工作环境。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mareksuppa.com/til/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TIL: You can make HTTP requests without curl using Bash /dev/TCP</span><span class='zh'>今天学到：可以用 Bash 的 /dev/TCP 发起 HTTP 请求，无需 curl</span></a><span class="dom">mareksuppa.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bash can issue HTTP requests by opening a TCP connection with /dev/tcp and writing raw HTTP, avoiding curl.</span><span class='zh'>通过打开 /dev/tcp 的 TCP 连接并发送原始 HTTP 请求即可在 Bash 中完成网络请求，无需 curl。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bash can issue HTTP requests by opening a TCP connection with /dev/tcp and writing raw HTTP, avoiding curl. This technique is handy in minimal environments or quick checks, but it&#x27;s brittle, lacks features like redirects and timeouts, and requires manual response parsing.</span><span class='zh'>通过打开 /dev/tcp 的 TCP 连接并发送原始 HTTP 请求即可在 Bash 中完成网络请求，无需 curl。适合极简环境和快速诊断，但缺乏如重定向、超时等特性，需要手动解析响应。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sandimetz.com/blog/2016/1/20/the-wrong-abstraction" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Prefer duplication over the wrong abstraction (2016)</span><span class='zh'>宁可重复，也别用错的抽象</span></a><span class="dom">sandimetz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Duplicating code can keep intent clear when an abstraction would misfit.</span><span class='zh'>在抽象不合适时，重复实现能保持意图清晰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Duplicating code can keep intent clear when an abstraction would misfit. Premature, poorly fitting abstractions hurt maintainability; the post argues that clarity beats clever architecture and that refactoring can come later when the need is real.</span><span class='zh'>在抽象不合适时，重复实现能保持意图清晰。过早的、与场景不符的抽象会降低可维护性；文章主张清晰胜过花哨的架构，真正需要时再进行重构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>There are no instances in ATProto</span><span class='zh'>ATProto 中不存在实例概念</span></a><span class="dom">overreacted.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ATProto’s architecture is often misunderstood as having &#x27;instances&#x27;.</span><span class='zh'>ATProto 的架构常被误解为‘实例’概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ATProto’s architecture is often misunderstood as having &#x27;instances&#x27;. The author argues there are no such isolated &#x27;instances&#x27; in ATProto because federation uses accounts and servers differently, emphasizing interoperability and same-identity across servers. This reframes how developers should think about hosting and moderation in ATProto.</span><span class='zh'>ATProto 的架构常被误解为‘实例’概念。作者指出在 ATProto 的联邦模型里并不存在真正的‘实例’，强调跨服务器的互操作性和统一身份的重要性，这对开发者在托管与 moderation 上的思路有显著影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/mfts0/status/2070080422482977095" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hey Nico, you didn&#x27;t vibe code your data room but stole it from Papermark</span><span class='zh'>嘿 Nico，你没有为数据房写代码却从 Papermark 偷来</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An accusation that Nico didn’t code his data room but copied it from Papermark, highlighting the importance of provenance and originality in data tooling.</span><span class='zh'>有人指控 Nico 没有自己实现数据房的代码，而是抄袭自 Papermark，这凸显了数据工具中来源证明与署名的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An accusation that Nico didn’t code his data room but copied it from Papermark, highlighting the importance of provenance and originality in data tooling. The thread serves as a cautionary note on attribution, tooling trust, and the risks of reusing others&#x27; work without proper credit.</span><span class='zh'>有人指控 Nico 没有自己实现数据房的代码，而是抄袭自 Papermark，这凸显了数据工具中来源证明与署名的重要性。此事强调在工具生态中对原创性和 attribution 的信任风险，以及对他人工作未正确致谢的潜在后果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apertvs.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apertus – Open Foundation Model for Sovereign AI</span><span class='zh'>Apertus：面向主权 AI 的开放基础模型</span></a><span class="dom">apertvs.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apertus unveils an open foundation model designed for sovereign AI deployment with data sovereignty baked in.</span><span class='zh'>Apertus 发布一个开放的基础模型，支持主权 AI 的部署与数据自主管控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apertus unveils an open foundation model designed for sovereign AI deployment with data sovereignty baked in. It supports open weights and configurable safety controls to reduce dependence on centralized providers, enabling in-country hosting and governance. This matters for governments and organizations seeking transparency and autonomy in AI.</span><span class='zh'>Apertus 发布一个开放的基础模型，支持主权 AI 的部署与数据自主管控。提供开源权重和可配置的安全策略，旨在降低对大型厂商的依赖，使其能在本地或受监管环境中运行。此举对政府与重视数据主权与透明度的机构尤为重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</span><span class='zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</span></a><span class="dom">thesciverse.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. If confirmed, this could influence dietary guidelines and therapeutic strategies for neurodegenerative diseases.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。若再证实，这将影响膳食建议和神经退行性疾病的治疗策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foodwatch.org/en/eu-banned-pesticides-found-in-rice-tea-and-spices" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU-banned pesticides found in rice, tea and spices</span><span class='zh'>在米、茶和香料中发现欧盟禁用农药</span></a><span class="dom">foodwatch.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EU-banned pesticides were detected in staple foods like rice, tea, and spices, pointing to ongoing gaps in testing in the global supply chain.</span><span class='zh'>在米饭、茶、香料等日常食品中检出欧盟禁用农药，暴露供应链检测与进口监管的薄弱环节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EU-banned pesticides were detected in staple foods like rice, tea, and spices, pointing to ongoing gaps in testing in the global supply chain. The finding underscores the challenges of ensuring pesticide safety in food imports and the regulatory implications.</span><span class='zh'>在米饭、茶、香料等日常食品中检出欧盟禁用农药，暴露供应链检测与进口监管的薄弱环节。这一发现凸显全球食品贸易中确保农药安全的挑战与监管含义。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/institute/recursive-self-improvement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=anthropic.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>anthropic.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/institute/recursive-self-improvement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When AI Builds Itself: Our progress toward recursive self-improvement</span><span class='zh'>AI 自我构建：向递归自我提升推进的进展</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines progress in recursive self-improvement, a scenario where AI systems iteratively enhance themselves with reduced human input.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 概述递归自我提升的进展，即 AI 系统在尽量减少人类介入的情况下迭代自我改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines progress in recursive self-improvement, a scenario where AI systems iteratively enhance themselves with reduced human input. The discussion touches on safety, alignment, and governance challenges as capabilities scale.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 概述递归自我提升的进展，即 AI 系统在尽量减少人类介入的情况下迭代自我改进。讨论涉及能力提升带来的安全、对齐与治理挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/court-records-should-be-free" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/supreme-court-3b.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">192</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/court-records-should-be-free" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Court Records Should Be Free</span><span class='zh'>法院记录应免费开放</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EFF argues that court records should be freely accessible and machine-readable to improve transparency, research, and accountability.</span><span class='zh'>EFF 呼吁法院记录应免费向公众开放并可机器可读，以提升透明度、研究和问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EFF argues that court records should be freely accessible and machine-readable to improve transparency, research, and accountability. The movement pushes for open court data standards and reduces friction for journalists, researchers, and the public.</span><span class='zh'>EFF 呼吁法院记录应免费向公众开放并可机器可读，以提升透明度、研究和问责。倡导建立公开的法院数据标准，降低记者、研究者和公众的使用成本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/librepods-org/librepods" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Librepods: AirPods liberated</span><span class='zh'>Librepods：AirPods 自由化项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments.</span><span class='zh'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments. The project advocates for ownership and privacy but may raise legal, warranty, and security questions.</span><span class='zh'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。该项目倡导拥有权与隐私，但也可能带来法律、保修与安全方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/28224" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex logging bug may write TBs to local SSDs</span><span class='zh'>Codex 日志记录漏洞可能将 TB 数据写入本地 SSD</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An issue in Codex&#x27;s logging subsystem can cause terabytes of data to be written to local SSDs, risking disk exhaustion on developer machines.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的日志记录子系统存在漏洞，可能把 TB 级数据写入本地 SSD，风险是磁盘空间耗尽。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An issue in Codex&#x27;s logging subsystem can cause terabytes of data to be written to local SSDs, risking disk exhaustion on developer machines. This highlights the fragility of client-side logging in AI-assisted tooling and the need for safer defaults and rate-limiting. The incident underscores the importance of robust storage safeguards when using code-generation copilots locally.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的日志记录子系统存在漏洞，可能把 TB 级数据写入本地 SSD，风险是磁盘空间耗尽。此事暴露了在 AI 辅助工具中进行本地日志记录的脆弱性，需更安全的默认设置和速率限制。强调在使用代码生成助手进行本地开发时加强存储保护的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/samsch/0d1f3d3b4745d778f78b230cf6061452" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Stop Using JWTs</span><span class='zh'>停止使用 JWTs</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concise critique of JSON Web Tokens, highlighting common misuses like improper storage, algorithm pitfalls, and relying on long-lived tokens.</span><span class='zh'>简要批评 JSON Web Token 的常见误用，如存储不当、算法陷阱和对长期令牌的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concise critique of JSON Web Tokens, highlighting common misuses like improper storage, algorithm pitfalls, and relying on long-lived tokens. The piece advocates safer authentication patterns and modern alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>简要批评 JSON Web Token 的常见误用，如存储不当、算法陷阱和对长期令牌的依赖。文中提倡采用更安全的认证模式和新兴替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/baidu/Unlimited-OCR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Unlimited OCR: One-shot long-horizon parsing</span><span class='zh'>Unlimited OCR：一次性长时域解析</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unlimited OCR introduces one-shot long-horizon parsing for documents, enabling robust recognition and parsing over long content in a single pass.</span><span class='zh'>Unlimited OCR 提出一次性对长篇文档进行解析的能力，提升单次完成对长文本的识别与解析的鲁棒性与效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unlimited OCR introduces one-shot long-horizon parsing for documents, enabling robust recognition and parsing over long content in a single pass. This advancement improves efficiency for enterprise document processing and hints at broader capabilities for scalable OCR in long-form workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Unlimited OCR 提出一次性对长篇文档进行解析的能力，提升单次完成对长文本的识别与解析的鲁棒性与效率。该进展有望显著优化企业级文档处理，并推动长文档工作流中的 OCR 能力扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pg_durable" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>pg_durable: Microsoft open sources in-database durable execution</span><span class='zh'>pg_durable：微软开源在数据库内的持久执行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft open-sourced pg_durable, a project aiming to provide durable, in-database execution for PostgreSQL.</span><span class='zh'>微软开源 pg_durable，目标是在 PostgreSQL 中实现持久化的数据库内执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft open-sourced pg_durable, a project aiming to provide durable, in-database execution for PostgreSQL. It tackles fault tolerance for long-running analytics and complex workflows by keeping computation results persistent within the database layer.</span><span class='zh'>微软开源 pg_durable，目标是在 PostgreSQL 中实现持久化的数据库内执行。该项目通过在数据库层持久化计算结果，提升长时间分析与复杂工作流的容错性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/c0dejedi/nbd-vram" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Use your Nvidia GPU&#x27;s VRAM as swap space on Linux</span><span class='zh'>在 Linux 上将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 用作交换空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Linux tool exposes Nvidia GPU VRAM as a swap device via NBD, letting systems swap to GPU memory under memory pressure.</span><span class='zh'>一款 Linux 工具通过 NBD 将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 暂时作为交换空间使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Linux tool exposes Nvidia GPU VRAM as a swap device via NBD, letting systems swap to GPU memory under memory pressure. It&#x27;s a niche hack that can extend usable RAM on GPUs with large VRAM but performance depends on workload and memory bandwidth. Not a replacement for main RAM, but a clever hack for specific workloads.</span><span class='zh'>一款 Linux 工具通过 NBD 将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 暂时作为交换空间使用。对于显存较大的显卡来说，在内存压力时可以延迟将数据写入磁盘，但它并非主 RAM 的替代品，性能取决于 workload 与带宽。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K2.7-Code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K2.7-Code: open-source coding model with better token efficiency</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K2.7-Code：开源编码模型，令牌效率更高</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kimi K2.7-Code is an open-source coding model optimized for token efficiency, enabling more cost-effective code generation and longer prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K2.7-Code 是一个强调高令牌利用率的开源编码模型，降低代码生成的成本并支持更长的输入。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kimi K2.7-Code is an open-source coding model optimized for token efficiency, enabling more cost-effective code generation and longer prompts. By improving token usage, it aims to close the gap with proprietary models while staying transparent and community-driven. The release underscores a growing trend toward more efficient, open-source AI tooling for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K2.7-Code 是一个强调高令牌利用率的开源编码模型，降低代码生成的成本并支持更长的输入。它在提升效率的同时保持透明与社区驱动，回应了开源 AI 工具日益增长的需求。此举反映了开发者生态中对更高性价比模型的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/29045" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Desktop spawns 1.8 GB Hyper-V VM on every launch, even for chat-only use</span><span class='zh'>Claude Desktop 启动时会开启一个 1.8 GB 的 Hyper-V 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Desktop launches a 1.8 GB Hyper-V virtual machine on every start, even when users only chat.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Desktop 启动时会开启一个 1.8 GB 的 Hyper-V 虚拟机，即使仅用于聊天也如此。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Desktop launches a 1.8 GB Hyper-V virtual machine on every start, even when users only chat. The heavyweight virtualization increases startup time and resource use, raising questions about desktop AI app design and user experience.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Desktop 启动时会开启一个 1.8 GB 的 Hyper-V 虚拟机，即使仅用于聊天也如此。这种重型虚拟化增加了启动时间和资源消耗，引发对桌面 AI 应用设计与用户体验的质疑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cyberpapiii/chipotlai-max" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chipotlai Max</span><span class='zh'>Chipotlai Max</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rio de Janeiro&#x27;s &quot;homegrown&quot; LLM appears to be a merge of an existing model</span><span class='zh'>里约热内卢的‘本土化’大模型似乎是现有模型的合并</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch.</span><span class='zh'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch. This provenance has implications for licensing, attribution, and how the model behaves in practice.</span><span class='zh'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。这对许可、署名和实际表现等方面都可能产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/devenjarvis/lathe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Lathe – Use LLMs to learn a new domain, not skip past it</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Lathe——用大模型深入学习新领域，而非走马观花</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lathe uses LLMs to thoroughly learn a new domain rather than skimming it, enabling deeper understanding and reproducible workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Lathe 通过大模型深入学习一个新领域，而不是走马观花，从而实现更深的理解和可重复的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lathe uses LLMs to thoroughly learn a new domain rather than skimming it, enabling deeper understanding and reproducible workflows. This approach highlights how AI can augment genuine knowledge acquisition, not just search or prompt tailoring.</span><span class='zh'>Lathe 通过大模型深入学习一个新领域，而不是走马观花，从而实现更深的理解和可重复的工作流。这显示了 AI 能力在真正的知识获取方面的潜力，而不仅仅是搜索或提示微调。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenKnowledge is an open-source knowledge-management app designed as an AI-first alternative to Obsidian and Notion.</span><span class='zh'>OpenKnowledge 是一个开源知识管理应用，定位为 Obsidian/Notion 的 AI 优先替代方案，强调本地优先的数据存储、AI 辅助的笔记与链接，以及对数据隐私的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenKnowledge is an open-source knowledge-management app designed as an AI-first alternative to Obsidian and Notion. It emphasizes local-first data, AI-assisted notes and linking, and user control over privacy.</span><span class='zh'>OpenKnowledge 是一个开源知识管理应用，定位为 Obsidian/Notion 的 AI 优先替代方案，强调本地优先的数据存储、AI 辅助的笔记与链接，以及对数据隐私的控制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/plbrault/youre-the-os" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A game where you&#x27;re an OS and have to manage processes, memory and I/O events</span><span class='zh'>你就是操作系统的游戏：管理进程、内存与 I/O 事件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This game puts you in the role of an OS kernel, forcing you to schedule processes, manage memory, and handle I/O events.</span><span class='zh'>玩家扮演操作系统内核，需完成进程调度、内存管理与 I/O 事件处理等任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This game puts you in the role of an OS kernel, forcing you to schedule processes, manage memory, and handle I/O events. It crystallizes core concepts of OS design and can serve as a hands-on lab for students and hobbyists to grasp how systems orchestrate tasks.</span><span class='zh'>玩家扮演操作系统内核，需完成进程调度、内存管理与 I/O 事件处理等任务。这款游戏将操作系统设计的核心概念变成直观的实践，适合作为学生和爱好者的动手实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/luke8086/gentleos32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GentleOS – Classic operating system with a lovely retro GUI</span><span class='zh'>GentleOS——带复古 GUI 的经典操作系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GentleOS is a hobbyist OS that recreates a classic desktop feel with a charming retro GUI.</span><span class='zh'>GentleOS 是一个爱好者层面的操作系统，带有迷人的复古GUI，重现经典桌面风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GentleOS is a hobbyist OS that recreates a classic desktop feel with a charming retro GUI. It showcases how lightweight systems can still provide a productive developer experience while leaning into nostalgia; a potential teaching platform for OS fundamentals and UI design. It&#x27;s a reminder that well-crafted UIs can coexist with modern abstractions in niche projects.</span><span class='zh'>GentleOS 是一个爱好者层面的操作系统，带有迷人的复古GUI，重现经典桌面风格。它展示了轻量级系统如何在保持怀旧感的同时提供高效的开发体验，亦可作为操作系统基础和界面设计的教学平台。精心设计的界面仍能在小众项目中与现代抽象共存。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ValveSoftware/GameNetworkingSockets/issues/398" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Major P2P issues in Israel and possibly other Middle East countries</span><span class='zh'>以色列及其他中东国家的P2P主要问题</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A discussion highlights major P2P networking issues affecting multiplayer in Israel, with potential spillover to neighboring Middle East countries.</span><span class='zh'>该讨论聚焦在以色列及可能波及邻近中东地区的P2P网络问题，影响多人游戏的对等连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A discussion highlights major P2P networking issues affecting multiplayer in Israel, with potential spillover to neighboring Middle East countries. Core problems likely involve ISP policies, restrictive NAT traversal, and platform-specific security scaffolding that disrupt peer-to-peer connections. These issues matter for game developers and any real-time app relying on P2P networking.</span><span class='zh'>该讨论聚焦在以色列及可能波及邻近中东地区的P2P网络问题，影响多人游戏的对等连接。问题核心可能与运营商策略、NAT穿透难题以及安全策略对等连接的限制相关。对依赖P2P的游戏开发者和实时应用来说，这些挑战尤为关键。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/06/06/top_stories_06062026'><span class='en'>Jun 6</span><span class='zh'>6月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>276</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431461' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>133</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-48660267" data-id="48660267" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="270" data-comments="76" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660267" data-time="1782310458">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nubjs/nub" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Nub – A Bun-like all-in-one toolkit for Node.js</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Nub——一个 Bun 风格的 Node.js 全能工具包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nub is a Bun-like toolkit for Node.js, providing a single binary with ecosystem utilities, bundling, package management, and more.</span><span class='zh'>Nub 将 Node.js 开发环境整合成一个 Bun 风格的工具包，包含运行时、包管理和常用开发工具，简化本地环境。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nub is a Bun-like toolkit for Node.js, providing a single binary with ecosystem utilities, bundling, package management, and more. If adopted, it could reshape local dev environments and reduce dependency friction for Node developers.</span><span class='zh'>Nub 将 Node.js 开发环境整合成一个 Bun 风格的工具包，包含运行时、包管理和常用开发工具，简化本地环境。若获得广泛采用，可能降低依赖冲突、提升上手速度，并推动 Node 生态的工具链整合。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tensorzero/tensorzero" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI OSS tool repo goes archived over night after raising $7.3M Seed</span><span class='zh'>AI 开源工具仓库融资后一夜之间归档</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI OSS project’s repository was archived overnight after a $7.3M seed round.</span><span class='zh'>在完成730万美元种子轮融资后，一款 AI 开源工具的代码仓库在一夜之间被归档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI OSS project’s repository was archived overnight after a $7.3M seed round. This highlights how funding does not guarantee long-term maintenance or governance in open-source projects and raises questions about sustainability and stewardship. It illustrates the fragility of OSS ecosystems when strategic priorities shift.</span><span class='zh'>在完成730万美元种子轮融资后，一款 AI 开源工具的代码仓库在一夜之间被归档。这反映了资金并不能确保长期维护与治理，并引发对开源生态系统在战略优先级变化时的可持续性与托管的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alibaba/open-code-review" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open Code Review – An AI-powered code review CLI tool</span><span class='zh'>Open Code Review：一个 AI 驱动的代码评审命令行工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Open Code Review is a CLI tool that uses AI to assist code reviews, potentially accelerating feedback cycles and surfacing issues developers might miss.</span><span class='zh'>Open Code Review 提供一个 AI 驱动的代码评审 CLI 工具，或将加速评审流程并发现开发者易错之处。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Open Code Review is a CLI tool that uses AI to assist code reviews, potentially accelerating feedback cycles and surfacing issues developers might miss. It reflects a broader trend of AI copilots embedded directly into tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Open Code Review 提供一个 AI 驱动的代码评审 CLI 工具，或将加速评审流程并发现开发者易错之处。此类工具代表开发者工具向终端 AI 助手的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google/copybara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google copybara: moving code between repositories</span><span class='zh'>Google Copybara：跨代码库迁移代码的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation. It helps teams maintain consistent workflows when moving assets, enabling cleaner separation of projects and easier collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。它帮助团队在跨仓库迁移时保持工作流的一致性，使项目分离更清晰、协作更高效。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepreinforce-ai/Ornith-1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0: self-improving open-source models for agentic coding</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0：具备代理能力的自我改进开源模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0 pushes open-source models toward self-improvement for agentic coding.</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0 将开源模型推向自我改进，聚焦具备代理能力的编码任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0 pushes open-source models toward self-improvement for agentic coding. The project explores mechanisms for models to refine their behavior and tooling autonomously, reducing human-labeled overhead. If successful, it could accelerate autonomous programming tasks and bring more capable AI-assisted development to the community.</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0 将开源模型推向自我改进，聚焦具备代理能力的编码任务。它尝试让模型自主优化行为和工具使用，降低人工标注的开销。一旦成熟，可能显著加速自动化编程和 AI 辅助开发的能力落地。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project shows Pico W can act as a USB Wi-Fi adapter, enabling a host computer to use the Pico as a network interface.</span><span class='zh'>该项目展示了 Pico W 作为 USB 无线网卡工作，使主机可以通过 Pico 的 USB 端口获得网络接口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project shows Pico W can act as a USB Wi-Fi adapter, enabling a host computer to use the Pico as a network interface. It is a handy trick for hobbyists building tiny, portable networks.</span><span class='zh'>该项目展示了 Pico W 作为 USB 无线网卡工作，使主机可以通过 Pico 的 USB 端口获得网络接口。展示了 RP2040 的 USB gadget 能力，为便携网络和离线共享等场景提供 DIY 方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/krauseler/muxcard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muxcard, a DIY credit card size computer</span><span class='zh'>Muxcard：一个自制的信用卡大小计算机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Muxcard is a DIY, credit-card-sized computer project that aims to be a portable, hackable development board.</span><span class='zh'>Muxcard 是一个自制的信用卡大小的计算机项目，旨在提供便携且可黑客化的开发板。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Muxcard is a DIY, credit-card-sized computer project that aims to be a portable, hackable development board. It emphasizes compact form factor while offering entry-level tooling and expandability for hobbyists. The project fits into the ongoing trend of pocketable computing devices for learning and prototype hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Muxcard 是一个自制的信用卡大小的计算机项目，旨在提供便携且可黑客化的开发板。其在紧凑体积的同时提供入门级的工具链与扩展性，面向爱好者进行学习与原型开发。这一设计体现了便携计算设备的持续热潮。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rxi/microui" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MicroUI – A tiny, portable, immediate-mode UI library written in ANSI C</span><span class='zh'>MicroUI：用 ANSI C 编写的微型、便携即时模式 UI 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MicroUI is a tiny, portable immediate-mode UI library written in ANSI C.</span><span class='zh'>MicroUI 是一个用 ANSI C 编写的微型、便携的即时模式 UI 库，体积极小、便于嵌入式和跨平台使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MicroUI is a tiny, portable immediate-mode UI library written in ANSI C. Its small footprint makes it attractive for embedded and cross-platform projects, and immediate-mode style can simplify rendering loops and minimize state management. It&#x27;s a handy option for lightweight UI when dependencies matter.</span><span class='zh'>MicroUI 是一个用 ANSI C 编写的微型、便携的即时模式 UI 库，体积极小、便于嵌入式和跨平台使用。即时模式的实现简化了渲染循环，降低了状态管理复杂性，适合对依赖要求很高的轻量 UI 场景。</span></p>

It highlights practical considerations like networking, kernel tuning, and software stacks for high throughput.</span><span class='zh'>该搭建指南演示如何在 AMD Strix Halo 硬件上构建低延迟 RDMA 集群，适用于运行大模型等 AI 工作负载。内容涵盖网络、内核调优及高吞吐的软件栈等实操要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/coreutils" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Coreutils for Windows</span><span class='zh'>Windows 版 Coreutils</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Coreutils for Windows brings GNU utilities like ls and cp to Windows native, streamlining cross-platform scripting without relying on WSL or Cygwin.</span><span class='zh'>Windows 版 Coreutils 将 GNU 工具（如 ls、cp）原生带到 Windows，简化跨平台脚本和开发工作，无需依赖 WSL 或 Cygwin。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Coreutils for Windows brings GNU utilities like ls and cp to Windows native, streamlining cross-platform scripting without relying on WSL or Cygwin.</span><span class='zh'>Windows 版 Coreutils 将 GNU 工具（如 ls、cp）原生带到 Windows，简化跨平台脚本和开发工作，无需依赖 WSL 或 Cygwin。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved. The discussion highlights ongoing data governance and privacy challenges in deploying code-generating AI, where guarding private or proprietary content is critical.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。此议题凸显了代码生成模型在数据治理和隐私保护方面的持续挑战，以及对私有或机密内容的防护需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pewdiepie-archdaemon/odysseus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Odysseus – self-hosted AI workspace</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus — 自托管的 AI 工作区</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Odysseus provides a self-hosted AI workspace for teams to manage models and experiments locally, emphasizing privacy, reproducibility, and control.</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus 提供一个面向团队的自托管 AI 工作区，便于在本地管理模型与实验，强调隐私、可重复性和对环境的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Odysseus provides a self-hosted AI workspace for teams to manage models and experiments locally, emphasizing privacy, reproducibility, and control. It appeals to developers seeking to avoid vendor lock-in and data leaving their environment.</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus 提供一个面向团队的自托管 AI 工作区，便于在本地管理模型与实验，强调隐私、可重复性和对环境的控制。适合希望避免厂商锁定与数据外流的开发者。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Optocam Zero demonstrates a compact, Pi Zero-based digital camera built entirely from off-the-shelf parts.</span><span class='zh'>Optocam Zero 展示了一个基于 Pi Zero 的小巧数字相机，用的是现成组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Optocam Zero demonstrates a compact, Pi Zero-based digital camera built entirely from off-the-shelf parts. The project shows how far a tiny board can go with modular components, trading off image quality and battery life for accessibility and affordability.</span><span class='zh'>Optocam Zero 展示了一个基于 Pi Zero 的小巧数字相机，用的是现成组件。这个开源硬件项目证明了在极小的单板机上拼装成像设备的可行性，同时也暴露了成像质量和电池寿命等权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A quirky GitHub gist proposes a workaround for MacBook Neo cursor lag by forcing a single pixel update every 10 seconds. The hack illustrates how developers tinker with OS rendering to squeeze perceived smoothness, revealing the fragility of UI performance on new hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的 GitHub 速记提出了一个修复 MacBook Neo 光标卡顿的变通方法：每 10 秒强制刷新一个像素。此类 hacks 展现了开发者在新硬件上为提升视觉流畅性而进行的微调，也暴露了 UI 性能的脆弱性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Adam-CAD/CADAM" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Adam (YC W25) – Open-Source AI CAD</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Adam（YC W25）——开源 AI CAD</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Adam is an open-source AI-powered CAD tool from YC&#x27;s W25 batch.</span><span class='zh'>Adam 是 YC W25 阵营推出的开源 AI CAD 工具，意在把 AI 辅助设计引入常用 CAD 工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Adam is an open-source AI-powered CAD tool from YC&#x27;s W25 batch. It aims to bring AI-assisted design into standard CAD workflows, with transparency and community governance through open source. This could accelerate feature development and lower the bar for researchers and hobbyists to experiment with AI in design.</span><span class='zh'>Adam 是 YC W25 阵营推出的开源 AI CAD 工具，意在把 AI 辅助设计引入常用 CAD 工作流。开源带来的透明性与社区治理可能加速功能迭代，降低研究者和爱好者在设计中尝试 AI 的门槛。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community project demonstrates smart routing that selects among Claude, Codex, and Cursor in real time, optimizing for speed and cost per task.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目演示在 Claude、Codex 与 Cursor 之间实现实时智能路由，按任务选择最合适的模型以优化速度和成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project demonstrates smart routing that selects among Claude, Codex, and Cursor in real time, optimizing for speed and cost per task. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I reverse-engineered the world maps of Test Drive III (1990 DOS game) to produce a machine-readable map dataset.</span><span class='zh'>我逆向还原了 1990 年 DOS 游戏 Test Drive III 的世界地图，生成了可机器读取的地图数据。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gooey is a GPU-accelerated UI framework for Zig, offering high-performance rendering while staying language-friendly.</span><span class='zh'>Gooey 是面向 Zig 的 GPU 加速 UI 框架，提供高性能渲染并保持语言友好性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BohemiaInteractive/CWR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bohemia Interactive: Cold War Assault Remastered Source Code on GitHub</span><span class='zh'>Bohemia Interactive：Cold War Assault Remastered 源代码在 GitHub 开源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bohemia Interactive released the Cold War Assault Remastered source code on GitHub, inviting modders and researchers to examine the engine and tools.</span><span class='zh'>Bohemia Interactive 将 Cold War Assault Remastered 的源代码发布到 GitHub，供社区研究引擎与工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bohemia Interactive released the Cold War Assault Remastered source code on GitHub, inviting modders and researchers to examine the engine and tools. This mirrors a broader trend of open-sourcing legacy game code to spur experimentation, while raising questions about licensing and attribution.</span><span class='zh'>Bohemia Interactive 将 Cold War Assault Remastered 的源代码发布到 GitHub，供社区研究引擎与工具。此举反映了开源遗留游戏代码以推动探索的趋势，同时也引发版权与署名方面的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/open-energy-transition/open-tyndp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU Open Sources Ten-Year Network Development Planning Tools</span><span class='zh'>欧盟开源十年网络发展规划工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU open-sources its Ten-Year Network Development Planning tools to help model future energy networks with greater transparency and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将十年网络发展规划工具开源，提升对未来能源网络的建模、透明度与互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU open-sources its Ten-Year Network Development Planning tools to help model future energy networks with greater transparency and interoperability. The release enables policymakers, grid operators, and researchers to collaboratively assess grid expansion scenarios as renewables scale.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将十年网络发展规划工具开源，提升对未来能源网络的建模、透明度与互操作性。此举将帮助政策制定者、输配电运营商和研究人员在可再生能源扩张场景下进行协同评估。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Demonstrates building a Matter-enabled Wi‑Fi light bulb using Rust on the Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showcasing a secure, memory-safe IoT path. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ogulcancelik/herdr" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Herdr: Agent multiplexer that lives in your terminal</span><span class='zh'>Herdr：驻留在终端中的代理多路复用器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr is a terminal-based agent multiplexer that lets you run and coordinate multiple agents in a single shell session.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 是一个在终端运行的代理多路复用器，允许在一个 shell 会话中同时管理和协同多个代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Herdr is a terminal-based agent multiplexer that lets you run and coordinate multiple agents in a single shell session. It enables modular agent orchestration with shared I/O, reducing context switching for power users.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 是一个在终端运行的代理多路复用器，允许在一个 shell 会话中同时管理和协同多个代理。它支持模块化编排、共享输入输出，降低高强度用户的上下文切换成本。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Paca introduces a lightweight Jira alternative tailored for human-AI collaboration, simplifying issue tracking while enabling AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Paca 提供一个面向人机协作的轻量级 Jira 替代方案，简化问题跟踪并支持 AI 辅助工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/overflowy/make-look-scanned" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Make PDFs look scanned (CLI or in the browser via WASM)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让 PDF 看起来像扫描件的工具（CLI 或 WASM 浏览器端）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tool that makes PDFs appear scanned, available as a command-line interface or in the browser via WebAssembly.</span><span class='zh'>这是一款能够把 PDF 渲染成看起来像扫描件的工具，提供 CLI 版本和 WASM 浏览器端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tool that makes PDFs appear scanned, available as a command-line interface or in the browser via WebAssembly. This can be useful for preserving the paper-like aesthetic, testing OCR, or creating consistent visuals for archiving.</span><span class='zh'>这是一款能够把 PDF 渲染成看起来像扫描件的工具，提供 CLI 版本和 WASM 浏览器端。对于需要保留纸质效果、测试 OCR 或统一档案外观的场景很实用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/HelixDB/helix-db/tree/main" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: HelixDB – A graph database built on object storage</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：HelixDB——一个建立在对象存储上的图数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HelixDB is a graph database designed atop object storage, offering scalable, cost-effective graph workloads by decoupling storage from compute.</span><span class='zh'>HelixDB 是一个建立在对象存储之上的图数据库，通过将存储与计算分离实现可扩展、成本友好的图工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HelixDB is a graph database designed atop object storage, offering scalable, cost-effective graph workloads by decoupling storage from compute. It explores data model, indexing strategies, and performance trade-offs, highlighting a novel architecture for graphs at scale.</span><span class='zh'>HelixDB 是一个建立在对象存储之上的图数据库，通过将存储与计算分离实现可扩展、成本友好的图工作负载。文章探讨数据模型、索引策略与性能权衡，呈现适用于大规模图数据的新架构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kekcleader/FreeOberon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FreeOberon – Open-Source, Cross-Platform, Free Pascal/Turbo Pascal-Like Language</span><span class='zh'>FreeOberon：开源、跨平台的 Oberon 风格语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FreeOberon presents an open-source, cross-platform take on the Oberon language, offering Pascal-like syntax and a compact toolchain.</span><span class='zh'>FreeOberon 提供开源、跨平台的 Oberon 语言实现，保留 Pascal 风格的语法与紧凑的工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FreeOberon presents an open-source, cross-platform take on the Oberon language, offering Pascal-like syntax and a compact toolchain. It aims to rekindle interest in language design from the Niklaus Wirth era while staying usable on modern systems. The project is of interest to language designers and hobbyists exploring minimal, robust toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>FreeOberon 提供开源、跨平台的 Oberon 语言实现，保留 Pascal 风格的语法与紧凑的工具链。旨在唤起人们对尼古拉斯·沃尔特时代语言设计的兴趣，同时在现代系统上保持可用。对于语言设计者和热衷于极简且稳健工具链的爱好者有一定吸引力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bun open-sources a PR to add shared-memory threading to JavaScriptCore, signaling stronger multicore capabilities for Bun&#x27;s runtime and potentially affecting how browsers and JS engines handle concurrency.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 为 JavaScriptCore 增加共享内存线程的 PR 进入开源阶段，标志着 Bun 运行时对多核并发能力的增强。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bun open-sources a PR to add shared-memory threading to JavaScriptCore, signaling stronger multicore capabilities for Bun&#x27;s runtime and potentially affecting how browsers and JS engines handle concurrency.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 为 JavaScriptCore 增加共享内存线程的 PR 进入开源阶段，标志着 Bun 运行时对多核并发能力的增强。若合并，开发者在并发 JavaScript 任务时将受益于更好的性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KVarN: Native vLLM backend for KV-cache quantization by Huawei</span><span class='zh'>KVarN：华为的本地 vLLM 后端实现 KV 缓存量化</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KVarN is a native backend for vLLM, focused on quantizing KV cache to speed up inference and reduce memory footprint.</span><span class='zh'>KVarN 是一个本地化的 vLLM 后端，针对 KV 缓存进行量化优化，提升推理速度并降低显存占用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KVarN is a native backend for vLLM, focused on quantizing KV cache to speed up inference and reduce memory footprint. This work aims to improve large language model deployments in Huawei&#x27;s ecosystem by making caching more efficient.</span><span class='zh'>KVarN 是一个本地化的 vLLM 后端，针对 KV 缓存进行量化优化，提升推理速度并降低显存占用。该实现有助于在华为生态中更高效地部署大语言模型，提升缓存效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zdk/lowfat" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Lowfat – pluggable CLI filter that saved 91.8% of my LLM tokens</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Lowfat – 可插拔的 CLI 过滤器，节省 91.8% 的 LLM 令牌</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lowfat is a pluggable CLI filter that dramatically reduces LLM token usage by trimming input before prompting.</span><span class='zh'>Lowfat 是一个可插拔的 CLI 过滤器，通过在提示前对输入进行裁剪，显著降低 LLM 令牌消耗，降低成本并提升响应速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lowfat is a pluggable CLI filter that dramatically reduces LLM token usage by trimming input before prompting. It helps cut costs and speed up interactions in LLM workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Lowfat 是一个可插拔的 CLI 过滤器，通过在提示前对输入进行裁剪，显著降低 LLM 令牌消耗，降低成本并提升响应速度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/themartiano/luz" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I wrote a C++ ray tracer from scratch without AI</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 我从零开始用 C++ 写了一个光线追踪器（没有 AI）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on showcase of a pure C++ ray tracer built from zero, likely implementing basic path tracing or ray casting with BVH, demonstrating fundamentals of graphics render pipelines without ML crutches.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用纯 C++ 从零开始编写光线追踪器的实战演示，可能实现了基本的路径追踪或光线投射和 BVH 等优化，展示无 ML 依赖的渲染管线基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on showcase of a pure C++ ray tracer built from zero, likely implementing basic path tracing or ray casting with BVH, demonstrating fundamentals of graphics render pipelines without ML crutches. It’s a reminder that high-performance, readable rendering code remains a valuable craft.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用纯 C++ 从零开始编写光线追踪器的实战演示，可能实现了基本的路径追踪或光线投射和 BVH 等优化，展示无 ML 依赖的渲染管线基础。它提醒人们，高性能、可读的渲染代码依然是一门宝贵的技艺。</span></p>

The project demonstrates minimalist OS design and retro computing techniques, offering a hands-on look at kernel, drivers, and tooling for older hardware.</span><span class='zh'>GentleOS 展示了两款面向老旧32位与16位 PC 的业余操作系统。这个项目通过极简化的 OS 设计与复古计算技术，展示了内核、驱动和工具链在旧硬件上的实现与学习价值。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Streambed is an open-source tool that streams PostgreSQL data into Iceberg on S3, including support for the Postgres wire protocol.</span><span class='zh'>Streambed 是一款开源工具，可将 PostgreSQL 数据流式写入 S3 上的 Iceberg，并支持 Postgres wire 协议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Streambed is an open-source tool that streams PostgreSQL data into Iceberg on S3, including support for the Postgres wire protocol. It simplifies real-time analytics architectures by enabling seamless data movement from transactional databases to a data lake format.</span><span class='zh'>Streambed 是一款开源工具，可将 PostgreSQL 数据流式写入 S3 上的 Iceberg，并支持 Postgres wire 协议。它通过实现从事务数据库到数据湖格式的数据无缝移动，简化了实时分析架构。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The LispE interpreter discussion contrasts FEXPRs and vtables, explaining how LispE handles dynamic evaluation and function calls.</span><span class='zh'>本文对 LispE 解释器中的 FEXPR 与 vtable 进行对比，解释其在动态求值和函数调用方面的实现方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The LispE interpreter discussion contrasts FEXPRs and vtables, explaining how LispE handles dynamic evaluation and function calls. It sheds light on design choices that balance expressiveness, performance, and memory use.</span><span class='zh'>本文对 LispE 解释器中的 FEXPR 与 vtable 进行对比，解释其在动态求值和函数调用方面的实现方式。解析这些设计取舍如何在表达能力、性能和内存消耗之间取得平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dmtrKovalenko/ffs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>F* file system – file search that reads SSD directly bypassing OS kernel</span><span class='zh'>F* 文件系统——直接读取SSD以绕过内核的文件搜索</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>F* is a file system project that reads SSDs directly to perform fast searches, bypassing the OS kernel.</span><span class='zh'>F* 文件系统通过直接读取 SSD 来实现快速搜索，绕过操作系统内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>F* is a file system project that reads SSDs directly to perform fast searches, bypassing the OS kernel. It trades portability and broad compatibility for speed and custom indexing, raising questions about stability and security.</span><span class='zh'>F* 文件系统通过直接读取 SSD 来实现快速搜索，绕过操作系统内核。它以提升速度和自定义索引为代价，牺牲了一定的可移植性与安全性，引发对稳定性与安全性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/garethgeorge/backrest" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Backrest – a web UI and orchestrator for restic backup</span><span class='zh'>Backrest：restic 备份的网页界面与编排工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Backrest provides a web UI and orchestrator for restic backups, making configuration, scheduling, and monitoring easier.</span><span class='zh'>Backrest 提供了一个用于 restic 备份的网页界面和编排工具，简化了配置、调度与监控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Backrest provides a web UI and orchestrator for restic backups, making configuration, scheduling, and monitoring easier. It offers a centralized view for multi-host backups and can reduce operational overhead. A practical boost for teams relying on restic in modern workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Backrest 提供了一个用于 restic 备份的网页界面和编排工具，简化了配置、调度与监控。它使跨主机的备份管理更加直观，并通过集中式视图提升可靠性与协作效率。对于依赖 restic 的团队而言，能显著降低运维负担。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DatanoiseTV/ember-hackernews" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ember, a native iOS Hacker News reader I built around accessibility</span><span class='zh'>Ember：我为无障碍设计打造的原生 iOS Hacker News 阅读器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ember is a native iOS Hacker News reader focused on accessibility. It prioritizes VoiceOver compatibility, large tap targets, and clean reading modes, showing how accessible-first design can broaden who can consume tech news on mobile.</span><span class='zh'>Ember 是一款原生 iOS 的 Hacker News 阅读器，聚焦无障碍体验。它优先考虑屏幕阅读器兼容性、易点击区域和简洁的阅读模式，展示了以无障碍为先的设计如何让更多人访问科技新闻。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cajal-technologies/talos" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Talos – Open-source WASM interpreter for Lean</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Talos——基于 Lean 的开源 WASM 解释器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talos is an open-source WASM interpreter implemented in Lean, emphasizing safety and potential formal verification benefits.</span><span class='zh'>Talos 是用 Lean 编写的开源 WASM 解释器，强调安全性与潜在的形式化验证优势，展示了在函数式语言中实现 WASM 运行时的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talos is an open-source WASM interpreter implemented in Lean, emphasizing safety and potential formal verification benefits. It demonstrates a WASM runtime in a functional language and may influence future tooling for verification-focused ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Talos 是用 Lean 编写的开源 WASM 解释器，强调安全性与潜在的形式化验证优势，展示了在函数式语言中实现 WASM 运行时的可能性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DDecoene/WebBaseIII" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WebBase-III – dBASE III rebuilt in the browser with its own interpreter</span><span class='zh'>Swsim：软件化 SIM 卡</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Swsim presents a software SIM card approach, allowing SIM credentials to be managed in software rather than a physical SIM.</span><span class='zh'>Swsim 提供软件化 SIM 卡方案，使 SIM 证书在软件中管理，而非物理 SIM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Swsim presents a software SIM card approach, allowing SIM credentials to be managed in software rather than a physical SIM. The project explores implications for carrier provisioning, device flexibility, and security risk in software-defined SIMs.</span><span class='zh'>Swsim 提供软件化 SIM 卡方案，使 SIM 证书在软件中管理，而非物理 SIM。该项目探讨对运营商配置、设备灵活性以及软件定义 SIM 的安全风险的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proton GE’s rebasing onto Proton 11 signals improved compatibility and performance for Windows games on Linux via Steam Play.</span><span class='zh'>GloriousEggroll 将 Proton 重基到 Proton 11，带来对 Linux 上 Steam Play 的更多游戏兼容性与性能提升。</span></p>

The update reflects ongoing community-driven improvements that broaden the Linux gaming landscape and reduce compatibility frictions.</span><span class='zh'>GloriousEggroll 将 Proton 重基到 Proton 11，带来对 Linux 上 Steam Play 的更多游戏兼容性与性能提升。这类社区驱动的持续改进正在扩展 Linux 游戏生态，降低兼容性摩擦。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1308</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/signalblur/exifsmugglingpoc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Exif Smuggling (2025)</span><span class='zh'>Exif 媒体元数据走私攻击</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Exif Smuggling demonstrates how EXIF metadata can be abused to smuggle payloads or bypass filters.</span><span class='zh'>Exif 媒体元数据走私攻击演示了如何利用 EXIF 元数据携带数据或绕过筛选。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Exif Smuggling demonstrates how EXIF metadata can be abused to smuggle payloads or bypass filters. It underscores the need for stringent metadata sanitization in image-handling pipelines and vigilant validation of uploaded media.</span><span class='zh'>Exif 媒体元数据走私攻击演示了如何利用 EXIF 元数据携带数据或绕过筛选。提醒在图片处理管线中对元数据进行严格清洗和有效性校验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jumpserver/jumpserver" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JumpServer: Open-Source Privileged Access Management</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer：开源特权访问管理工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>JumpServer emerges as an open-source Privileged Access Management solution.</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer 作为开源特权访问管理解决方案渐入人心，提供集中访问控制、会话记录与审计等能力，帮助关键系统的安全管控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>JumpServer emerges as an open-source Privileged Access Management solution. It offers centralized access control, session recording, and auditing for critical systems, presenting a cost-effective alternative to proprietary PAM. For security teams, it lowers barriers to strong PAM practices and integration with existing infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer 作为开源特权访问管理解决方案渐入人心，提供集中访问控制、会话记录与审计等能力，帮助关键系统的安全管控。对安全团队而言，它提供了一更具性价比的替代方案，易于与现有基础设施整合。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mondaycom/HATCHA" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Captcha proves you&#x27;re human. HATCHA proves you&#x27;re not</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 证明你是人，HATCHA 证明你不是</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CAPTCHA proves you&#x27;re human; HATCHA aims to prove you&#x27;re not, offering a new angle on bot verification.</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 用以证明你是人，HATCHA 则试图证明你不是，为机器人验证带来新的思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CAPTCHA proves you&#x27;re human; HATCHA aims to prove you&#x27;re not, offering a new angle on bot verification. The project explores more robust or alternative challenges to distinguish humans from automated agents, with implications for security and accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 用以证明你是人，HATCHA 则试图证明你不是，为机器人验证带来新的思路。该项目探讨更强韧的挑战或替代方案来区分人类与自动化程序，并对安全性与可访问性带来影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ponytail – make your AI agent think like the laziest senior dev in the room</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail：让 AI 助手像最懒的资深开发者一样思考</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults.</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults. It aims to improve reliability and speed of AI-assisted coding.</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。目标是提升 AI 辅助编码的可靠性与速度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/evanklem/polypore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agentic coding deserves more than a chat box bolted onto VS Code</span><span class='zh'>代理式编码不应只是挂在 VS Code 上的一个聊天框</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agentic coding argues for more than a chatbot embedded in VS Code.</span><span class='zh'>代理式编码的主张不仅限于在 VS Code 中嵌入一个聊天机器人，而是让 AI 代理真实参与编码工作，并提升用户体验、可靠性与治理，避免只靠聊天界面的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agentic coding argues for more than a chatbot embedded in VS Code. It advocates building autonomous AI agents that actively assist coding tasks, with better UX, reliability, and governance to avoid brittle, chat-only interfaces. The idea pushes for a richer integration of agency in development tools.</span><span class='zh'>代理式编码的主张不仅限于在 VS Code 中嵌入一个聊天机器人，而是让 AI 代理真实参与编码工作，并提升用户体验、可靠性与治理，避免只靠聊天界面的脆弱性。这一思路推动开发工具更充分地集成“代理能力”。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/YusufB5/ASCILINE" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Asciline – real-time ASCII video rendering engine</span><span class='zh'>Asciline：实时 ASCII 视频渲染引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asciline is a real-time ASCII video renderer that converts video frames into ASCII art on the fly, enabling lightweight, terminal-friendly previews or retro aesthetics.</span><span class='zh'>Asciline 是一个实时的 ASCII 视频渲染引擎，能够在传输过程中将视频帧转换成 ASCII 艺术，适合轻量级终端预览和复古风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asciline is a real-time ASCII video renderer that converts video frames into ASCII art on the fly, enabling lightweight, terminal-friendly previews or retro aesthetics. The project demonstrates efficient streaming and clever text-based rendering techniques that can run with minimal dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>Asciline 是一个实时的 ASCII 视频渲染引擎，能够在传输过程中将视频帧转换成 ASCII 艺术，适合轻量级终端预览和复古风格。该项目展示了高效流式处理和文本渲染技术，依赖极少。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rvaiya/keyd" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A key remapping daemon for Linux</span><span class='zh'>用于 Linux 的按键重映射守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keyd is a low-level Linux daemon that remaps keys and creates advanced keyboard macros, useful for ergonomic setups and gaming.</span><span class='zh'>Keyd 是一个 Linux 上的底层守护进程，用于重新映射按键并创建高级键盘宏，适合人体工学和游戏等场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keyd is a low-level Linux daemon that remaps keys and creates advanced keyboard macros, useful for ergonomic setups and gaming. It runs in user space, offering predictable latency and flexibility for customizing input across X11/Wayland.</span><span class='zh'>Keyd 是一个 Linux 上的底层守护进程，用于重新映射按键并创建高级键盘宏，适合人体工学和游戏等场景。它在用户空间运行，提供可预测的延迟和跨 X11/Wayland 的自定义输入能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/remysucre/prela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Prela – Purely Algebraic Relation Combinators</span><span class='zh'>Prela：纯代数关系组合子</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Prela provides a purely algebraic framework for relation combinators, enabling declarative construction and manipulation of relations without procedural code.</span><span class='zh'>Prela 提供一个纯代数框架，用于关系的组合子，强调以声明式方式构造与操作关系，而非依赖过程性代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Prela provides a purely algebraic framework for relation combinators, enabling declarative construction and manipulation of relations without procedural code. The approach could influence databases, program analysis, and symbolic computation by offering a solid mathematical foundation.</span><span class='zh'>Prela 提供一个纯代数框架，用于关系的组合子，强调以声明式方式构造与操作关系，而非依赖过程性代码。这一思路有望为数据库、程序分析与符号计算提供坚实的数学基础。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ilbertt/bun-sqlgen" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Bun-sqlgen – Type-safe raw SQL for Bun, no ORM</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Bun-sqlgen——为 Bun 提供类型安全的原始 SQL，无需 ORM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bun-sqlgen offers type-safe raw SQL for Bun without ORM, enabling compile-time checks and safer data access while staying lightweight in Bun’s JS/TS environment.</span><span class='zh'>Bun-sqlgen 提供 Bun 的类型安全原始 SQL，摆脱 ORM 依赖，利用编译时检查提升数据访问安全性，同时保持在 Bun 的 JavaScript/TypeScript 生态中轻量高效。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bun-sqlgen offers type-safe raw SQL for Bun without ORM, enabling compile-time checks and safer data access while staying lightweight in Bun’s JS/TS environment.</span><span class='zh'>Bun-sqlgen 提供 Bun 的类型安全原始 SQL，摆脱 ORM 依赖，利用编译时检查提升数据访问安全性，同时保持在 Bun 的 JavaScript/TypeScript 生态中轻量高效。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TorchCodec 0.14 adds HDR video decoding support on CPU and CUDA, expanding hardware-accelerated media processing for PyTorch workflows, plus a fast WAV decoder to speed audio paths.
