<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/schlae/IBM_MCGA" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IBM MCGA Gate Array Reverse Engineering</span><span class='zh'>IBM MCGA 门阵列逆向工程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Details a reverse‑engineering effort on IBM&#x27;s MCGA gate array, with insights into the chip&#x27;s architecture and reconstruction notes.</span><span class='zh'>披露对 IBM MCGA 门阵列的逆向工程工作，包含架构要点和还原笔记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Details a reverse‑engineering effort on IBM&#x27;s MCGA gate array, with insights into the chip&#x27;s architecture and reconstruction notes. This work serves as a practical case study in vintage hardware analysis, useful for retrocomputing enthusiasts and hardware researchers.</span><span class='zh'>披露对 IBM MCGA 门阵列的逆向工程工作，包含架构要点和还原笔记。此类研究为复古硬件爱好者和硬件验证者提供了一个真实世界的案例，展示早期芯片分析的方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zaydmulani09/mnemo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mnemo – local-first AI memory layer for any LLM (Rust, SQLite,petgraph)</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo：本地优先的 AI 记忆层，面向任意大型语言模型（Rust、SQLite、petgraph）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mnemo provides a local-first memory layer for LLMs, enabling on-device or local-then-sync memory storage using Rust, SQLite, and petgraph.</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo 为 LLM 提供本地优先的记忆层，利用 Rust、SQLite 和 petgraph 实现本地存储并可与云端同步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mnemo provides a local-first memory layer for LLMs, enabling on-device or local-then-sync memory storage using Rust, SQLite, and petgraph. This approach improves privacy and latency and can reduce reliance on external vector databases. It could become a building block for offline-capable AI apps.</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo 为 LLM 提供本地优先的记忆层，利用 Rust、SQLite 和 petgraph 实现本地存储并可与云端同步。提高隐私与低延迟，降低对外部向量数据库的依赖，有望成为离线 AI 应用的基础组件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alebal123bal/khadas_yolov8n_multithread" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Dual YOLOv8n UAV Detection on RK3588S at 42 FPS Using NPU</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 RK3588S 上使用 NPU 的双 YOLOv8n 无人机检测，42 FPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/80084" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UUID: NewV7() always generates a UUID with 7000 on browsers (Golang)</span><span class='zh'>UUID NewV7 在浏览器上总是生成 7000 的值</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Go issue reports that uuid.NewV7 produces browser-specific anomalies, always returning 7000 on browsers.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Golang 的问题报告指出在浏览器环境下 uuid.NewV7 的结果出现异常，总是返回 7000。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Go issue reports that uuid.NewV7 produces browser-specific anomalies, always returning 7000 on browsers. The note underscores cross-environment id generation quirks and the importance of robust implementation testing.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Golang 的问题报告指出在浏览器环境下 uuid.NewV7 的结果出现异常，总是返回 7000。此发现强调跨环境生成唯一标识符时需要仔细测试与一致性考虑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/cznic/sqlite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sqlite: A CGo-free port of SQLite/SQLite3</span><span class='zh'>sqlite：无 CGo 的 SQLite/SQLite3 端口</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>sqlite: A CGO-free port of SQLite/SQLite3 describes a CGO-free embedding of SQLite, reducing binary dependencies and making it easier to build in environments without CGo.</span><span class='zh'>该项目提供了一个无 CGo 的 SQLite/SQLite3 端口，便于在不依赖 cgo 的环境中嵌入数据库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>sqlite: A CGO-free port of SQLite/SQLite3 describes a CGO-free embedding of SQLite, reducing binary dependencies and making it easier to build in environments without CGo. There may be trade-offs in performance or compatibility, but it simplifies cross-language use.</span><span class='zh'>该项目提供了一个无 CGo 的 SQLite/SQLite3 端口，便于在不依赖 cgo 的环境中嵌入数据库。它降低了二进制依赖与跨编译难度，但可能在性能或兼容性上有权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/prostomarkeloff/ohbin" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ohbin – uv wrapper for installing tools from GitHub</span><span class='zh'>Ohbin：GitHub 工具安装的 uv 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ohbin is a uv wrapper that simplifies installing tools from GitHub, making it easier to fetch and manage GitHub releases.</span><span class='zh'>Ohbin 是一个用于从 GitHub 安装工具的 uv 封装器，使获取和管理 GitHub 发布变得更简单。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ohbin is a uv wrapper that simplifies installing tools from GitHub, making it easier to fetch and manage GitHub releases. It reflects a broader trend toward streamlined, scriptable tooling in the developer ecosystem and raises considerations around provenance and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Ohbin 是一个用于从 GitHub 安装工具的 uv 封装器，使获取和管理 GitHub 发布变得更简单。它体现了开发者生态中对简化、可脚本化工具的趋势，同时也引发关于来源可靠性和安全性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/react/react/pull/36173" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Port React Compiler to Rust</span><span class='zh'>将 React 编译器移植到 Rust</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pull request proposes porting the React compiler to Rust, aiming for faster builds and memory safety.</span><span class='zh'>一个 PR 提议将 React 编译器移植到 Rust，目标是提升编译速度并增强内存安全。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pull request proposes porting the React compiler to Rust, aiming for faster builds and memory safety. If adopted, the move could influence how critical tooling in the React ecosystem evolves, potentially accelerating compile times for large apps.</span><span class='zh'>一个 PR 提议将 React 编译器移植到 Rust，目标是提升编译速度并增强内存安全。若被采纳，这一做法可能影响 React 生态中关键工具链的演化，帮助大型应用的构建时间更短。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AnthonySturdy/micro-radar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Micro Radar: a tiny open-source flight radar for your desk</span><span class='zh'>微型雷达：桌面上的极小开源飞行雷达</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact, open-source flight radar you can run on a desk, letting hobbyists and researchers visualize nearby air traffic from your home.</span><span class='zh'>一个小型开源飞行雷达，放在桌面即可观测周围的航空流量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact, open-source flight radar you can run on a desk, letting hobbyists and researchers visualize nearby air traffic from your home. The project combines affordable sensors, open firmware, and approachable hardware to democratize access to radar-based sensing and data exploration.</span><span class='zh'>一个小型开源飞行雷达，放在桌面即可观测周围的航空流量。该项目结合了廉价传感器、开源固件和易于获取的硬件，降低了雷达探测的门槛，便于爱好者与研究者进行数据探索。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Showcasing Autofit2, an end-to-end pipeline for multilingual text classification, aiming to simplify data prep, model training, and evaluation across languages.</span><span class='zh'>展示 Autofit2——一个面向多语言文本分类的端到端流水线，简化数据准备、模型训练与评估流程，适用于多语言场景的实际应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Showcasing Autofit2, an end-to-end pipeline for multilingual text classification, aiming to simplify data prep, model training, and evaluation across languages. It provides a reusable workflow that accelerates building and evaluating multilingual classifiers for real-world apps.</span><span class='zh'>展示 Autofit2——一个面向多语言文本分类的端到端流水线，简化数据准备、模型训练与评估流程，适用于多语言场景的实际应用。该项目提供了可复用的工作流，帮助快速建立与评估跨语言分类模型。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tool that helps you self-host Django projects more easily, likely via Docker or automation scripts, streamlining deployment on personal servers or private clouds.</span><span class='zh'>Djevops 让 Django 的自托管变得更简单，可能通过 Docker 或自动化脚本实现，使个人服务器或私有云上的部署更高效、维护更便捷。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AnalogCyberNuke/RE-Verse-2026-Slides/blob/main/Reverse26.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>(Re//Verse 2026) Taxonomy and Deobfuscation of a Real World Binary Obfuscator [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>RE//Verse 2026：真实世界二进制混淆器的分类与去混淆 [PDF]</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The talk presents a taxonomy of real-world binary obfuscation techniques and practical deobfuscation methods, helping defenders understand how obfuscation hides intent and how to reverse engineer obscured code.</span><span class='zh'>该演讲对真实世界二进制混淳技术进行了分类，并给出去混淳的实用方法，帮助安全研究者理解混淆如何隐藏意图并提供系统化的逆向分析思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The talk presents a taxonomy of real-world binary obfuscation techniques and practical deobfuscation methods, helping defenders understand how obfuscation hides intent and how to reverse engineer obscured code.</span><span class='zh'>该演讲对真实世界二进制混淳技术进行了分类，并给出去混淳的实用方法，帮助安全研究者理解混淆如何隐藏意图并提供系统化的逆向分析思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/loadingalias/rscrypto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rscrypto, pure-Rust crypto with industry leading public benches</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Rscrypto，纯 Rust 的密码学库，具行业领先的公开基准</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rscrypto is a pure-Rust cryptography library with public benchmarks, aiming for transparent, verifiable performance.</span><span class='zh'>Rscrypto 是一个纯 Rust 的密码学库，提供公开的基准以实现透明可核验的性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rscrypto is a pure-Rust cryptography library with public benchmarks, aiming for transparent, verifiable performance. The project appeals to security-conscious developers who want reliable crypto in Rust without vendor-specific optimizations.</span><span class='zh'>Rscrypto 是一个纯 Rust 的密码学库，提供公开的基准以实现透明可核验的性能。该项目面向重视安全性的开发者，追求在 Rust 中获得可靠且可验证的加密功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vladignatyev/brain-map-skill" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Skill for your agent to visualize your gbrain and Obsidian</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让你的代理可视化你的“gbrain”和 Obsidian 的技能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN project that adds a skill allowing an AI agent to visualize its own reasoning (the &#x27;gbrain&#x27;) and map it into an Obsidian notebook.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目新增一个技能：让代理能够可视化自己的推理过程（gbrain），并将结果映射到 Obsidian 中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN project that adds a skill allowing an AI agent to visualize its own reasoning (the &#x27;gbrain&#x27;) and map it into an Obsidian notebook. The tool lowers the barrier to introspection, aiding debugging and collaboration with human teammates. It showcases practical thinking visualization for AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目新增一个技能：让代理能够可视化自己的推理过程（gbrain），并将结果映射到 Obsidian 中。该工具降低了对齐与调试的门槛，便于与人类团队协作。展示了在 AI 工作流中进行思维可视化的实用路径。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://gist.github.com/alurm/2ca14be134d719fe7431217a6b18d91e" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gist.github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gist.github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/alurm/2ca14be134d719fe7431217a6b18d91e" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A generic dynamic array in C that stores no capacity and needs no struct</span><span class='zh'>一个在 C 语言中实现的通用动态数组：不存储容量、也不需要结构体</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A clever C technique for a dynamic array that doesn&#x27;t store capacity or require a separate struct, using macro tricks and pointer arithmetic.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个在 C 语言中实现的通用动态数组，它既不存储容量也不需要单独的结构体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A clever C technique for a dynamic array that doesn&#x27;t store capacity or require a separate struct, using macro tricks and pointer arithmetic. It demonstrates how to package generic data structures with minimal overhead, at the cost of portability and clarity. The example invites reconsideration of how metadata is managed in low-level code.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个在 C 语言中实现的通用动态数组，它既不存储容量也不需要单独的结构体。通过宏和指针运算实现通用性与低开销，便于在对性能要求极高的场景中使用。这一技巧也引发对元数据如何在底层实现中管理的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ascorbic/cirrus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cirrus: ATProto Personal Data Server That Runs on Cloudflare Workers</span><span class='zh'>Cirrus：在 Cloudflare Workers 上运行的 ATProto 个人数据服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cirrus is a personal data server for AT Protocol that runs on Cloudflare Workers, enabling users to host their own data with decentralized protocol support.</span><span class='zh'>Cirrus 是一个在 Cloudflare Workers 上运行的 AT Protocol 个人数据服务器，允许用户在边缘计算环境中托管自己的数据，并支持去中心化协议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cirrus is a personal data server for AT Protocol that runs on Cloudflare Workers, enabling users to host their own data with decentralized protocol support. It demonstrates feasibility and limits of edge runtimes for personal data sovereignty, including storage and privacy tradeoffs.</span><span class='zh'>Cirrus 是一个在 Cloudflare Workers 上运行的 AT Protocol 个人数据服务器，允许用户在边缘计算环境中托管自己的数据，并支持去中心化协议。它展示了边缘运行时在个人数据主权方面的可行性与局限性，以及存储和隐私权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/harihkk/Debug-Brief" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DebugBrief – turn debugging sessions into reports, no AI</span><span class='zh'>DebugBrief：将调试会话转化为报告，无需 AI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DebugBrief turns debugging sessions into readable reports without relying on AI, helping engineers document issues, steps, and fixes efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>DebugBrief 将调试会话整理成可读报告，无需依赖 AI，帮助工程师高效记录问题、步骤与修复。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DebugBrief turns debugging sessions into readable reports without relying on AI, helping engineers document issues, steps, and fixes efficiently. It streamlines post-mortems and onboarding for new maintainers.</span><span class='zh'>DebugBrief 将调试会话整理成可读报告，无需依赖 AI，帮助工程师高效记录问题、步骤与修复。提升事后总结和新维护者的上手效率。</span></p>
